## Topic 5: Non-uniform Circular Motion

### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A road is banked at an angle of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>30</mn></mrow> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math> to the horizontal for negotiating a curve of radius <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> <mo>m</mo> </mrow> </math>. At what velocity will a car experience no friction while negotiating the curve? Take <math> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>ms</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math > <mrow>  <mtext>54&#x00A0;km/hr</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math > <mrow>  <mtext>72&#x00A0;km/hr</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math > <mrow>  <mtext>36&#x00A0;km/hr</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math > <mrow>  <mtext>18&#x00A0;km/hr</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math > <mrow>  <mtext>36&#x00A0;km/hr</mtext></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For negotiating curve without friction <math> <mrow> <mtext>tan</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>rg</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>V</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msqrt> <mtext>rg</mtext> <mo>tan</mo> <mi>θ</mi> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

  <math > <mrow>  <mtext>=&#x00A0;10&#x00A0;m/s</mtext></mrow></math>

  <math > <mrow>  <mtext>=&#x00A0;36&#x00A0;km/hr</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is released from height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi></math> on a smooth curved surface which ends into a vertical loop of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math>, as shown. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></math>, then

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image4-b7b1532a-5cbd-466e-ac6d-af905a74b5bd.jpg" style="height: 115px; width: 200px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** the particle reaches the top of the loop with zero velocity
- **(B)** the particle reaches the top of the loop with a non-zero velocity
- **(C)** the particle breaks off at a height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>r</mi></math> from base
- **(D)** the particle breaks off at a height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) the particle breaks off at a height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

To reach the top of loop, particle must have a minimum speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math> at the bottom of the loop.

but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></math> , so the block won't reach the highest point of the track.

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The bob of the simple pendulum is given a vertically downward velocity of magnitude <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, as shown in the figure. The string can withstand a maximum tension equal to twice the weight of the bob and during motion the string breaks at an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>θ</mi></math> with the vertical. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>m</mi><mi>n</mi></mfrac></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> are smallest integers, then, the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi></math> is


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image33-6af253cd-e984-4fd4-904f-b108273cbf6f.jpg" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>
<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image34-a123c456-fd3d-4850-83b3-1ea8e04d6e0a.jpg" />

Let us assume that the length of the string is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="bold-italic"> </mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

In a circular motion of a particle, the tangential acceleration of the particle is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The radius of the circle is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. The particle was initially at rest. Time after which total acceleration of the particle makes an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>45</mn><mo>°</mo></math> with the radial acceleration is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>r</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>.

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> moves along the internal smooth surface of a vertical cylinder of the radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>. Find the force with which the particle acts on the cylinder wall if at the initial moment of time its velocity equals <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>. and forms an angle <math><mrow><mi>α</mi> </mrow> </math> with the horizontal.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>sin</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mrow></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The direction of the velocity of the particle may be shown as in the diagram. This velocity V<sub>0</sub> is resolved in two components. Here <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>cos</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math> component is tangential horizontal and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>sin</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is along the surface but vertically downwards. Here, if the particle exerts a force N on the surface of the cylinder, the cylinder will also apply equal and opposite force on the particle


              <u><strong><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0027045_ANs_.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 160px; height: 200px;" /></strong></u>


and this will provide a centripetal force to the particle.

So,   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">N</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">m</mtext><msup><mfenced><mrow><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>cos</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>/</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></math>

           <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>/</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></math>

or,   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">N</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A body moves in a circular path of the radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> with deceleration so that at any moment of time its tangential and normal acceleration are equal in magnitude. At the initial moment <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> , the velocity of the body is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> then the velocity of the body after it has moved <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math> at any time will be -

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>T</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>N</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>vdv</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>ds</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

(-ve because it is a retardation) and s is the distance. covered tangentially on circular path

on integrating we get

<math> <mrow> <munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <msub> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>v</mtext> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>dv</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>v</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>s</mtext> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>ds</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>log</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <msub> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>v</mtext> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>log</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>v</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>v</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msup> <mtext>e</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A solid body rotates an angle <math><mi>θ</mi></math> about a stationary axis according to the law <math><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> . What is the mean value of angular velocity (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>rad</mi><mo>&#xA0;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) over the time interval between <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the time till the body comes to rest?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> ...(i)

<math><mo>∴</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

As, <math><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (given)

<math><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

As, <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>s</mtext></math> is not possible, hence <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>s</mtext><mo>.</mo></math>

Substituting <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>s</mtext></math> in Equation (i), we get

<math><msub><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>r</mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>d</mtext></math>

and <math><msub><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Mean value of <math><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mtext>r</mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>d </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small coin of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>40</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is placed on the horizontal surface of a rotating disc. The disc starts from rest and is given a constant angular acceleration <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mtext> rad s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The coefficient of static friction between the coin and the disc is <math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mtext>s</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>/</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </math> and the coefficient of kinetic friction is <math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mtext>k</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0.5</mn> </mrow> </math>. The coin is placed at a distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> from the centre of the disc. The magnitude of the resultant force on the coin exerted by the disc just before it starts slipping on the disc is :

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0021287_Question_1.jpg" style="width: 175px; height: 179px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0021287_AnsExp_1.jpg" style="width: 167px; height: 130px;" />

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/666554412.JPG" style="height: 134px; width: 200px;" />

The friction force on coin just before coin is to slip will be : <math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mtext>s</mtext> </msub> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> </math>
Normal reaction on the coin ; N = mg
The resultant reaction by disk to the coin is

    <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <msqrt> <msup> <mtext>N</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>f</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo> <msqrt> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mtext>s</mtext> </msub> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> <msqrt> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msubsup> <mi>μ</mi> <mtext>s</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

    <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <msqrt> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.5 N</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

An aeroplane is flying at a velocity of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>900</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> loops a vertical circular loop. If the maximum force pressing the pilot against the seat is five times his weight, what would be the diameter (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>) of the loop? [ <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> ]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3125**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>900</mo><msup><mtext>km h</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

    <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>900</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>18</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

    <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mtext>250 </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0043533_Ans.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 156px; height: 140px;" />

the maximum force is at the bottom of the vertical circle

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mtext>max</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>r</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mtext>4</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">gr</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mtext>4</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">g</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mtext>250</mtext></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mfenced><mtext>10</mtext></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mtext>1562.5 </mtext><mo>m</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3125</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A point object moves on a circular path such that distance covered by it is given by function <math><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> meter ( <math><mi>t</mi></math> in second). The ratio of the magnitude of acceleration at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>. is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>2</mn></math> then the radius of the circle is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1 </mn><mtext>m</mtext></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>51</mn></msqrt><mtext> </mtext><mtext>m</mtext></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>51</mn></msqrt><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3 </mn><mtext>m</mtext></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>51</mn></msqrt><mtext> </mtext><mtext>m</mtext></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
at <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> s <math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>51</mn></msqrt><mi> </mi><mtext>m</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small ball of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mo>×</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> having a charge of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>μ</mi><mtext>C</mtext></math> is suspended by a string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. Another identical ball having the same charge is kept at the point of suspension. Determine the minimum horizontal velocity which should be imparted to the lower ball so that it can make a complete revolution.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 87px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 173px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 65px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>5.86 </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>5.86 </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If the ball has to just complete the circle then the tension must vanish at the topmost point, i.e.,T<sub>2</sub> = 0

From Newton&#39;s second law,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>T</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mtext>mg</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mn>i</mn></mfenced></math>

At the topmost point T<sub>2</sub> = 0,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>mg</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mtext>ii</mtext></mfenced></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0028371_Ans.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 160px; height: 224px;" />

From energy conservation,

Energy at lowest point = Energy at topmost point

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>mg2</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>ii</mtext><mtext>i</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>v</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>u</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mtext>g</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>v</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

From Eqn. (ii), <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>v</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>m</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mtext>v</mtext></mfenced></math>

From Eqns. (iv) and (v),

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>u</mtext><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mtext>g</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>m</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>7</mn> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>86</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A point <math><mtext>P</mtext></math> moves in a counter-clockwise direction on a circular path as shown in the figure. The movement of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>P</mtext></math> is such that it sweeps out a length <math><mtext>s</mtext><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mtext>t</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math> where <math><mtext>s</mtext></math> is in metre and <math><mtext>t</mtext></math> is in seconds. The radius of the path is <math><mn>27 </mn><mtext></mtext><mtext>m</mtext><mo>.</mo></math> The acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>P</mtext></math> when <math><mtext>t</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>s</mtext></math> is ____ <math><mrow><mrow><mtext>m</mtext></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">T</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">a</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">k</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">e</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>3.6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/965522fe-cf42-4e21-a133-8227b0e096f5.png" style="width:1.97986in;height:1.90694in" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **32.40**

**Detailed Solution:**

As <math><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>v</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mo mathvariant="normal">→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msubsup><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>t</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></msqrt></math>
At <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>s</mi></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mo mathvariant="normal">→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext>v</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>t</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mtext>t</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mtext>t</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>324</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>1053</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mtext>a</mtext></mrow><mo mathvariant="normal">→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>32.4 </mn><mi></mi><mtext>m</mtext><mo>/</mo><msup><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle moves in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></math> plane. The position vector of the particle at any time <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. The rate of change of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>θ</mi></math> at time <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> (where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> is the angle which its velocity vector makes with positive <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> -axis ) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>14</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>6</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>t</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><msub><mi>v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></math>

Differentiating with respect to time we get,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>sec</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The velocity and acceleration vectors of a particle undergoing circular motion are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mtext>i</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mtext>i</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mtext>j</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> respectively at an instant of time. What is the radius (in <math><mtext>m</mtext></math>) of the circle?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

It can be observed that the component of acceleration perpendicular to velocity is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mtext>Radius</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext mathvariant="italic">v</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">a</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">c</mtext></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A horizontal disk is rotating with angular velocity <math><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></math> about a vertical axis passing through its centre. A ball is placed at the centre of groove and pushed slightly. The velocity of the ball when it comes out of the groove-

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/111111sss.png" style="width: 150px; height: 148px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let us consider the motion of the ball with respect to disk

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/74910__11.JPG" style="width: 204px; height: 153px;" />

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/74910__11dddd.JPG" style="width: 204px; height: 172px;" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>r</mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>r</mi></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi>v</mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>v</mi></msubsup><mi>v</mi><mo>d</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac bevelled="true"><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msubsup><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>d</mo><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">→</mo></mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mrow><mi>Disk</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mrow><mi>Ball</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>Ground</mi></mrow></msub></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>120</mn><mo>°</mo></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The kinetic energy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>K</mi></math> of a particle moving along a circle of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> depends upon the distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>s</mi></math> , as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> . The force acting on the particle is-

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mfrac><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>R</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>s</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>s</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>s</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mi>m</mi></mfrac></msqrt></mfenced><mi>s</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mi>m</mi></mfrac></msqrt></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mi>m</mi></mfrac><mi>s</mi></math></p><p>The net force on the particle is</p><p><math><mi mathvariant="italic">F</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">d</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">d</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">s</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math></p><p><math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">s</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> stone tied at the end of a string <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> long is whirled along a vertical circle at a constant speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. What will be the tension in the string (in newton) at the bottom-most point?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **52**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Tension at the bottom of the circle,

<math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>52</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

When a ceiling fan is switched off, its angular velocity reduces to <math><mn>50</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">%</mi></math> of its initial value while it makes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>36</mn></math> rotations. If angular retardation of the fan is uniform, then how many more rotations will it make before coming to rest?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>18</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>12</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>36</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>48</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow>  <mtext>12</mtext></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math> ..........(i)

<math><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> .............(ii)

from (i) &amp; (ii) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>36</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>

</details>

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A stone of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> tied to a light inextensible string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mtext> m</mtext></math>  is whirling in a circular path of the radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> in the vertical plane. If the ratio of the maximum tension to the minimum tension in the string is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>. What is the speed (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) of stone at the highest point of the circle? (Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mtext>m s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> )

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **10**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0320804-2.jpg" style="width: 250px; height: 298px;" />

<math> <mrow> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>-</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>5 gL</mtext> </mrow> </math>

By energy conservation

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><msub><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>4 gL</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>3 gL</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mtext>10</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>v</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>10 m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A horizontal disk is rotating with angular velocity <math><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></math> about a vertical axis passing through its centre. A ball is placed at the centre of groove and pushed slightly. The velocity of the ball when it comes out of the groove-

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/111111sss.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 100px; height: 99px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let us consider the motion of the ball with respect to disk

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/74910__11.JPG" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 204px; height: 153px;" />

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/74910__11dddd.JPG" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 204px; height: 172px;" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>r</mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>r</mi></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi>v</mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>v</mi></msubsup><mi>v</mi><mo>d</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mfrac bevelled="true"><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msubsup><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>d</mo><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">→</mo></mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mrow><mi>Disk</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><msub><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mrow><mi>Ball</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>Ground</mi></mrow></msub></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>120</mn><mo>°</mo></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A stone hanging from a massless string of length <math><mn>15</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> is projected horizontally with speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The speed of the particle, at the point where the tension in the string is equal to the weight of the particle, is close to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#xA0;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>As, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math> we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>Also we know that</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>u</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>12</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>93</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8776;</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>
