### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Four identical uniform rods of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>6</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> each are welded their ends to form a square and then welded to a uniform ring having mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>4</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> and radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. The system is allowed to roll down a rough incline of inclination <math> <mrow> <mi>θ</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math>. ( where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi></math> is in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-07-23_at_3.18.40_PM.jpeg" style="width: 200px; height: 130px;" />

The force of friction between the system and the plane is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>6</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">g </mtext><mtext>downwards</mtext></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>6</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">g&#160;</mtext><mtext>upwards</mtext></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mtext>upwards</mtext></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>downwards</mtext></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mtext>upwards</mtext></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-07-23_at_3.18.59_PM.jpeg" style="width: 200px; height: 133px;" />

 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">M</mtext><msup><mfenced><mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">M</mtext><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mo mathvariant="italic">mR</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>20 kg m</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">M</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">m</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mtext mathvariant="italic">g</mtext><mtext> sin</mtext><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>4 </mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">M</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">m</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mtext mathvariant="italic">a</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">FR</mtext><mo>=</mo><mi>I</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">a</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac></mfenced></math>

Solving, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">a</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mtext>7</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"> g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>4 </mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">M</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">m</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">g</mtext><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">a</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>4 </mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">M</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">m</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mtext>12</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"> g</mtext><mo>-</mo><mtext>7</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"> g</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mn>7</mn></mfenced><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mtext>5</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"> g</mtext></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mtext>35</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"> g</mtext></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mtext>upwards</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q63. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is placed at the lowest point of a smooth vertical track of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>. In this position, the block is given a horizontal velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi></math> such that the block is just able to perform a complete vertical circular motion. The acceleration of block, when its velocity is vertical is</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/bbbbb.JPG" style="width: 200px; height: 187px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>3g</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>g</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The initial velocity of the block to undergo a complete vertical circular motion is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></msqrt></math></p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/bbbbb-11.JPG" style="width: 165px; height: 250px;" /></p><p>Let us assume that the speed of the block when it is moving in the vertical direction is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi></math>, then using conservation of mechanical energy we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mfenced><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></msqrt></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>2</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></msqrt></math></p><p>The tangential and centripetal accelerations of the block are</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mo>&#8595;</mo></mfenced></math> and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#160;</mo><mfenced><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8592;</mo></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>net</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small sphere of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> suspended by a thread is first taken aside so that the thread forms a right angle with the vertical and then released. The total acceleration of the sphere when it makes an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> with the vertical is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Between A and B, <math> <mrow> <mtext> mgL  cos </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴    </mo> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>gL  cos </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

Now, <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>r</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>g  cos  </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>                

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0014783_AnsExp_1_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 126px; height: 128px;" />

and <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>t</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>g </mtext> <mtext>sin </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴    </mo> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msqrt> <msubsup> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>t</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>r</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <msqrt> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>3</mn> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>θ</mi> </msqrt></mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q65. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A body of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> starts moving from rest at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn></math>, in a circular path of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. Its kinetic energy varies as a function of times as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>KE</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> Joules, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi></math> is in seconds. Then

**Options:**

- **(A)** Tangential acceleration = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 157px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** Power of all forces at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 249px; top: 39.2292px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** First-round is completed in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>.<div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 193px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(D)** Tangential force at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>.<div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 237px; top: -4.21528px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Power of all forces at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 249px; top: 39.2292px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8658;&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mtext>d</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">v</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>d</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">t</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mtext>m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mi>a</mi><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>N</mtext></math>

Power will be inverted only by tangential force

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><msub><mover><mi>F</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mover><mi>F</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>c</mtext></msub></mrow></mfenced><mtext>.</mtext><mover><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> since <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext><mtext>c</mtext></msub><mo>&#8869;</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mtext>&#160;&#38;</mtext><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#160;F</mtext><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow/></mfenced><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext><mo>=</mo><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext><mtext>t</mtext></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">t</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">t</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi></math>

</details>

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a particle in a uniform circular motion, the acceleration <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> on the circle of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math> is (here <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> is measured from the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>-axis)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mi>&theta; </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>sin </mtext> <mi>&theta; </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>sin </mtext> <mi>θ </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mi>θ </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mi>&theta; </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>sin </mtext> <mi>&theta; </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>sin </mtext> <mi>θ </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mi>θ </mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>**

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> is fixed at one end with a bob of mass <math><mn>100</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> and the string makes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mfrac><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> around a vertical axis through a fixed point. The angle of inclination of the string with vertical is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/74959_sol_CA.JPG" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 250px; height: 124px;" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi> </mi></math> .....(i)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> .....(ii)

So <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>r</mi></mrow><mi>g</mi></mfrac></math> ;

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>l</mi><mi mathvariant="script"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mi>g</mi></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mrow><msup><mi>ω</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mo> </mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>8</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is given a horizontal velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi></math>, from the point <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> on a smooth horizontal floor which has a vertical circular track at the end. <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is a semicircular track of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> in the vertical plane. If the path length <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>r</mi></math> and the particle returns to point <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>, then the initial speed of the particle is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image12-2907c7c9-57d2-4169-b6f5-c7016ce93daa.jpg" style="height: 147px; width: 180px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image13-55689c2e-7bba-4a38-912a-3bcded168ec6.jpg" style="height: 169px; width: 200px;" />

or, <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

as, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

From equation (1) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The bob of a pendulum of mass <math><mi>m</mi></math> and length <math><mi>L</mi></math> is displaced <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>°</mo></math> from the vertical and gently released. In order that the string may not break upon passing through the lowest point, its minimum strength must be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If <math><mi>v</mi></math> is velocity of the bob on reaching the lowest point, then <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi>L</mi></math>

To avoid breaking, strength of the string

<math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi> </mi><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is suspended by a light vertical inelastic string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> from a fixed support. At its equilibrium position, it is projected horizontally with a speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>6</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi></msqrt></math>. What is the ratio of the tension in the string in its horizontal position to when the particle is vertically above the point of support?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

For a given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>6</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi></msqrt></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0023824_AnsExp_2.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 326px; height: 155px;" />
At the horizontal position,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mtext>4 </mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">mg</mtext></math>                .......... (i)

At the highest point

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mtext>2 </mtext><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">mgT</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">mg</mtext></math>             ......... (ii)

From the above equations, we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is rotating about a vertical axis in the horizontal plane such that the angular velocity of the particle about the axis is constant and is equal to 1 rad/s. Distance of the particle from axis is given by <math><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>t</mi></math> where t stands for time. The speed of the particle as a function of time is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>β</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi></math> 

 <math><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>ω</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>ω</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  

 <math><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msubsup><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>

</details>

---

### Q72. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A long horizontal rod has a bead which can slide along its length, and initially it is at a distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> from the end <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> of the rod. The rod is set in angular motion about <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> with constant angular acceleration <math> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </math> in a gravity free space. If the coefficient of friction between the rod and the bead is <math> <mrow> <mi>μ</mi> </mrow> </math>, then the time after which the bead starts slipping is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>μ</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <msqrt> <mi>&alpha;</mi> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msqrt> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mi>&alpha;</mi> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** infinitesimal

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>μ</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>L</mi></math></p><p>When the bead starts slipping

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>f</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>L</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>L</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0010194_AnsExp1_1..jpg" style="width: 325px; height: 108px;" /></p>

</details>

---

### Q73. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A solid body rotates about a stationary axis so that its angular velocity depends on the rotational angle <math><mi>ϕ</mi></math> as <math><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>ϕ</mi></math> where <math><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> are positive constants. At the moment <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> find the time dependence of rotation angle

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>k</mi><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> <math><mi>&#969;</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></math> or <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></math>

Or <math><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

Or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>k</mi></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><msubsup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mn>0</mn><mi>&#981;</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>

Or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ln</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> <math><mi>&#981;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q74. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A ceiling fan rotates about its own axis with some angular velocity. When the fan is switched off, the angular velocity becomes <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup></math> of the original in time <math><mi>t</mi></math> and <math><mi>n</mi></math> revolutions are made in that time. The number of revolution made by the fan during the time interval between switch off and rest are (Angular retardation is uniform)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>32</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mi>n</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mi>n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>′</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>n</mi></math>

</details>

---
